### General Terms & Conditions:
- The Velocity will be Once per card during the offer period
- The offer period is 1st September to 30th September 2026. 
- The offer is valid on HDFC Credit / Debit Card EMI transactions only.
- EMI Offer is applicable on 6, 8, 9, 10, 12, 18-, 24, 36 & 48-months tenures.
- The offer is valid for selected card bins.
- Also Applicable on Brand EMI, Where there is NO CASHBACK/ DISCOUNT from the Brand
- Velocity once per card during offer period, no two offers can be clubbed
- One card One transaction is applicable during the offer period.
- One card is allowed against one invoice.
- Multiple bank offers cannot be availed of on a single invoice.
- The bank will charge interest under the bank EMI option as per applicable rates.
- The bank may charge processing fees or any other charges against EMI transactions.
- Vijay Sales has no role in the case of any additional charges levied by the bank. The customer has to directly connect with the bank for any kind of additional charge.
